Safe to run at any time — never modifies without permission. ([Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev](https://github.com/Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev/blob/HEAD/commands/electrobun-align.md) · ★5)

- **electrobun-feature**: Build a complete Electrobun feature end-to-end using the two-agent team — UI agent designs views and produces the RPC contract, backend agent implements bun-side wiring. Produces all renderer files plus updated bun entrypoint and electrobun.config.ts. ([Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev](https://github.com/Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev/blob/HEAD/commands/electrobun-feature.md) · ★5)
- **electrobun-init**: Scaffold a new Electrobun desktop app project from scratch. Creates full directory structure, package.json, electrobun.config.ts, tsconfig.json, and entry point files. - **electrobun-menu**: Generate an ApplicationMenu with File/Edit/View/Help items wired to application-menu-clicked events, plus optional Tray setup. Asks whether to forward events to a webview via RPC. ([Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev](https://github.com/Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev/blob/HEAD/commands/electrobun-menu.md) · ★5)

- **electrobun-rpc**: Introspect existing Electrobun bun-process and renderer files to generate a complete type-safe RPC schema. ([Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev](https://github.com/Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev/blob/HEAD/commands/electrobun-rpc.md) · ★5)
- **electrobun-sdlc**: Run the full 8-stage Electrobun SDLC pipeline for a feature — researcher, architect, planner, dev squad, QA engineer, test writer, alignment agent, docs agent. - **electrobun-setup**: Run immediately after electrobun init. Derives the project name from electrobun.config.ts (never assumes a hardcoded name), scans for existing .github infrastructure, then walks through a wizard to create CI/CD, release pipeline, test scaffold, docs, CLAUDE.md, AI code review, trust scoring, and pre-push hooks for a production-ready project. ([Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev](https://github.com/Dexploarer/plugin-electrobun-dev/blob/HEAD/commands/electrobun-setup.md) · ★5)

- **electrobun-wgpu**: Set up a WebGPU rendering window in an Electrobun project. Creates a GpuWindow with WGPUView, a minimal WGSL pass-through shader, render loop, and KEEPALIVE array. - **element.by-parameter-value**: Typed filter over the active document — find elements where a named parameter matches a value (or is null / not null). ([aware-aeco/aware](https://github.com/aware-aeco/aware/blob/HEAD/20-agents/aeco/architecture/revit-2026/commands/element.by-parameter-value.md) · ★7 · 2 copies)
- **element.list-by-classification**: List every element on the plan that is classified under the given ClassificationItem in the given ClassificationSystem. The audit primitive for "show me every IfcWall" or "every fire-rated assembly". ([aware-aeco/aware](https://github.com/aware-aeco/aware/blob/HEAD/20-agents/aeco/architecture/archicad-29/commands/element.list-by-classification.md) · ★7)
- **element.list-by-trade**: List elements in the active Allplan document filtered by **trade** (architecture / structure / MEP / civil) and, optionally, by a regex matched against the element's **layer name**. Returns `id`, `type`, `layer`, and `trade` per element — the audit primitive for "show me every structural element" or "every MEP object on a `*-DRAFT` layer". ([aware-aeco/aware](https://github.com/aware-aeco/aware/blob/HEAD/20-agents/aeco/architecture/allplan-2025/commands/element.list-by-trade.md) · ★7)
- **element.set-layer-by-pattern**: Move every element whose current layer name matches a regex onto a target layer. Used for cleaning imported models or normalizing DWG-derived geometry. ([aware-aeco/aware](https://github.com/aware-aeco/aware/blob/HEAD/20-agents/aeco/architecture/archicad-29/commands/element.set-layer-by-pattern.md) · ★7)
- **elements**: Each `## id` section adds its paragraphs to that command as notes, a `See also:` line as related commands and each `### title` with its `lua` block as an example, which runs in the tests and must end in `assert`s. A section naming several commands goes to each of them, before their own. The command reference in `docs/SCRIPTING.md` is generated from them. ([gilbertorconde/printCAD](https://github.com/gilbertorconde/printCAD/blob/HEAD/crates/workbenches/wb_inspect/src/commands/elements.md) · ★7)
- **elenchus**: Socratic discovery & idea refutation engine. Elicits lived pain, runs parallel ideation divergence (SCAMPER/TRIZ/personas), frames measurable outcomes, and conducts prior-art graveyard analysis before code is written. - **endurance**: Set or clear the long-running goal for this John session. Pinned into the system prompt by the SessionStart hook so it survives context compaction and stays visible across long ralph-loop runs. Use this command when the user says "let's run this in endurance mode", "set the endurance goal to ...", "/john:endurance ...", or starts any long-running shakedown / pipeline / build that should hold a finish line across many compactions. ([kitchen-engineer42/joharnessburg](https://github.com/kitchen-engineer42/joharnessburg/blob/HEAD/plugins/joharnessburg/commands/endurance.md) · ★9)
- **enemy**: Open an enemy lane session (enemies, bosses, final threat, concept art, 3D model and animation) ([twglhk/gamedev-agent-skills](https://github.com/twglhk/gamedev-agent-skills/blob/HEAD/commands/enemy.md))
- **eneo-commit**: Review the staged commit before it lands. Runs deterministic preflight + commit-message checks, invokes security-reviewer only on risky diffs, then commits through normal git hooks. ([CCimen/eneoplugin](https://github.com/CCimen/eneoplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/eneo-core/commands/eneo-commit.md))
- **eneo-discuss**: Socratic interview + scope lockdown for Deep-lane PRDs. Runs Pocock's interview script with the SuperClaude 90/70 confidence gate. Soft-requires plan mode; dispatches a read-only Explore subagent to verify assertions against the repo. ([CCimen/eneoplugin](https://github.com/CCimen/eneoplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/eneo-core/commands/eneo-discuss.md))
- **eneo-doctor**: Run environment diagnostics at any time. Prints the exact command to fix every issue it detects. First thing a new developer runs; first thing anyone runs when something feels off. ([CCimen/eneoplugin](https://github.com/CCimen/eneoplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/eneo-core/commands/eneo-doctor.md))
- **eneo-new**: Triage + entry point for Fast/Standard/Deep lanes. Classifies the change, writes .claude/state/current-task.json, and creates the right artifact (prompt / SPEC.md / PRD + GitHub issue) based on lane. Prompts only when classification is genuinely borderline. ([CCimen/eneoplugin](https://github.com/CCimen/eneoplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/eneo-core/commands/eneo-new.md))
- **eneo-peer-review**: Ask an outside peer reviewer (Codex if installed, else fresh-context Claude) to challenge the current diff or task. Plain-language input — the runner infers paths, focus, phase, and skepticism. Manual only; never an auto hook. Distinct from /eneo-verify gate 7 (automatic Borda) and /eneo-commit (commit-time safety). ([CCimen/eneoplugin](https://github.com/CCimen/eneoplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/eneo-review/commands/eneo-peer-review.md))
- **eneo-plan**: Turn an approved PRD into a tracer-bullet phased plan (Pocock template). Phase 1 is always the thinnest end-to-end path; phase count 3–6. Stop-hook validator enforces required sections. ([CCimen/eneoplugin](https://github.com/CCimen/eneoplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/eneo-core/commands/eneo-plan.md))
- **eneo-prune**: List stale skills (not triggered in 90 days) and recaps older than 6 months for archive decision. Never auto-deletes — always prints a review table and waits for the developer. ([CCimen/eneoplugin](https://github.com/CCimen/eneoplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/eneo-core/commands/eneo-prune.md))
- **eneo-recap**: Close the milestone. Only runs when all phases are shipped. Writes an agent-os-style milestone recap, closes the PRD issue with a recap link, archives the phases dir, and dispatches the learning-extractor subagent for skill candidates. ([CCimen/eneoplugin](https://github.com/CCimen/eneoplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/eneo-core/commands/eneo-recap.md))
- **eneo-ship**: Open a PR for the current phase via gh. Enforces tenancy/audit/PRD/phase metadata; Stop-hook validator fails the ship if any field is missing. ([CCimen/eneoplugin](https://github.com/CCimen/eneoplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/eneo-core/commands/eneo-ship.md))
- **eneo-start**: Resume the current plan and run its next incomplete phase via Wave → Checkpoint → Wave. Smart defaults; only prompts when there is real ambiguity. Emergency phase override via --phase. ([CCimen/eneoplugin](https://github.com/CCimen/eneoplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/eneo-core/commands/eneo-start.md))
- **eneo-verify**: Ratchet gate for the current phase — pyright strict, pytest, coverage, mutation ≥70%, audit-log completeness, tenancy smoke, conditional adversarial review on risky paths. Streams each gate as it runs. ([CCimen/eneoplugin](https://github.com/CCimen/eneoplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/eneo-core/commands/eneo-verify.md))
